  1329	  const value = frame.value;
  1330	  const slots: Slots = { ...baseSlots(frame), grammar: decl.grammar?.re };
     1	import {
     2	  type DeclCursor,
     3	  RELATION_READS,
     4	  defaultFindings,
     5	  derefNode,
     6	  descend,
     7	  resolveSelectorPath,
     8	  selectorRead,
     9	  shapesOf,
    10	} from "./engine.js";
    11	import type {
    12	  EqualsRuleDecl,
    13	  MessageTemplate,
    14	  NodeDecl,
    15	  NormalizerHookDecl,
    16	  Selector,
    17	  SubstrateBranch,
    18	  SurfaceDecl,
    19	} from "./vocabulary.js";
    20	
    21	/**
    22	 * THE DECLARATION GATE — closure, then immutability.
    23	 *
    24	 * A surface declaration is a LANGUAGE, and until this gate existed its
    25	 * shapes were checked by the type system while its NAMES AND PATHS were
    26	 * resolved at run time with nothing checking them. Every silent-failure
    27	 * finding of the 2026-08-06 design review was one unresolved reference:
    28	 * a value-class name that did not exist (the engine returned the value
    29	 * unvalidated), a selector path written in the documented-but-inert form
    30	 * (the rule never fired), a `channel` mark at a site nothing reads.
    31	 *
    32	 * So: a declaration that is not CLOSED does not become a surface. Every
    33	 * reference is resolved here, once, at load; an unresolved one throws
    34	 * before any document is ever validated. Fail-closed, loudly, at startup —
    35	 * never a no-op at validation time, which is the failure mode that hides.
    36	 *
    37	 * WHERE THE RESOLVING HAPPENS is the second lesson, learnt three rounds
    38	 * running. This gate used to keep its OWN model of which positions exist
    39	 * and what a selector reaches — a replica of the engine's walk — and every
    40	 * round found another place the replica had drifted from the original. It
    41	 * keeps none now. `descend` and `resolveSelectorPath` are the ENGINE's, the
    42	 * same calls its runtime selector walk makes, so what this gate certifies
    43	 * as reachable is reachable by construction. What is left here is the
    44	 * POLICY: an answer must exist, it must be readable for EVERY legal value,
    45	 * and it must exist on BOTH channels.
    46	 *
    47	 * The second half is `deepFreeze`: ADR-019 D4 calls the declaration "a
    48	 * frozen declaration object", and `Object.freeze` alone froze only the
    49	 * outermost object — the review mutated a live finding message through it.
    50	 * The whole tree is frozen here, so the claim and the bytes agree.
    51	 */
    52	
    53	/** A declaration that is not closed. Carries every problem, not the first
    54	 * — a maintainer fixing one reference should see the rest in the same
    55	 * breath rather than one per run. */
    56	export class SurfaceDeclarationError extends Error {
    57	  readonly problems: readonly string[];
    58	
    59	  constructor(problems: readonly string[]) {
    60	    super(
    61	      `the surface declaration is not closed (${String(problems.length)} unresolved reference(s)):\n` +
    62	        problems.map((problem) => `  - ${problem}`).join("\n"),
    63	    );
    64	    this.name = "SurfaceDeclarationError";
    65	    this.problems = problems;
    66	  }
    67	}
    68	
    69	/** The placeholder slots a message or a finding-path template may use —
    70	 * the engine's closed set (`renderMessage`). */
    71	const SLOTS = new Set([
    72	  "path",
    73	  "key",
    74	  "keyJson",
    75	  "value",
    76	  "valueRaw",
    77	  "valueJson",
    78	  "source",
    79	  "grammar",
    80	  "members",
    81	  "keys",
    82	  "label",
    83	  "ownerKey",
    84	]);
    85	
    86	const SELECTOR_HEADS = new Set(["$", "^"]);
    87	
    88	function slotProblems(template: MessageTemplate, where: string): string[] {
    89	  const problems: string[] = [];
    90	  for (const match of template.matchAll(/\{(\w+)\}/gu)) {
    91	    const slot = match[1] ?? "";
    92	    if (!SLOTS.has(slot)) {
    93	      problems.push(`${where}: unknown placeholder {${slot}} (the slot set is closed)`);
    94	    }
    95	  }
    96	  return problems;
    97	}
    98	
    99	/** A SELECTOR path: head `$` or `^`, dot-separated nonempty segments, `*`
   100	 * legal only as a whole segment. This is the check that kills the
   101	 * documented-but-inert `..` form — it now throws instead of resolving to
   102	 * nothing. */
   103	function selectorPathProblems(path: string, where: string): string[] {
   104	  const segments = path.split(".");
   105	  const head = segments[0] ?? "";
   106	  if (!SELECTOR_HEADS.has(head)) {
   107	    return [
   108	      `${where}: selector path ${JSON.stringify(path)} must start with "$" (the document root) ` +
   109	        `or "^" (the citing node's container); got ${JSON.stringify(head)}`,
   110	    ];
   111	  }
   112	  return segmentProblems(segments.slice(1), path, "selector", where);
   113	}
   114	
   115	/** A NORMALIZER operand path: `$`-rooted, dot-separated, with `*` legal as
   116	 * a whole segment (the `.*.` wildcard the hook resolver splits on). */
   117	function normalizerPathProblems(path: string, where: string): string[] {
   118	  const segments = path.split(".");
   119	  if (segments[0] !== "$") {
   120	    return [`${where}: normalizer path ${JSON.stringify(path)} must start with "$"`];
   121	  }
   122	  return segmentProblems(segments.slice(1), path, "normalizer", where);
   123	}
   124	
   125	/** Both path languages spell a segment the same way, because the engine
   126	 * reads both with the same descent. */
   127	function segmentProblems(segments: readonly string[], path: string, language: string, where: string): string[] {
   128	  const problems: string[] = [];
   129	  for (const segment of segments) {
   130	    if (segment === "") {
   131	      problems.push(`${where}: ${language} path ${JSON.stringify(path)} has an empty segment`);
   132	    } else if (segment.includes("*") && segment !== "*") {
   133	      problems.push(
   134	        `${where}: ${language} path ${JSON.stringify(path)} uses "*" inside a segment; ` +
   135	          `"*" is legal only as a whole segment`,
   136	      );
   137	    }
   138	  }
   139	  return problems;
   140	}
   141	
   142	/** A FINDING-PATH template is RENDERED, never walked: it may carry closed
   143	 * placeholder slots and must not carry selector syntax. */
   144	function findingPathProblems(path: string, where: string): string[] {
   145	  const problems = slotProblems(path, where);
   146	  const literal = path.replace(/\{\w+\}/gu, "");
   147	  if (literal.startsWith("$") || literal.startsWith("^") || literal.includes("*")) {
   148	    problems.push(
   149	      `${where}: finding path ${JSON.stringify(path)} uses selector syntax; ` +
   150	        `a finding path is rendered, not walked`,
   151	    );
   152	  }
   153	  return problems;
   154	}
   155	
   156	interface Context {
   157	  readonly surface: SurfaceDecl;
   158	  /** The document root as a declaration position — the base of every
   159	   * `$`-rooted path, and the engine's own. */
   160	  readonly root: DeclCursor;
   161	  readonly codes: ReadonlySet<string>;
   162	  readonly tags: string[];
   163	  /** Tags the ENGINE records reliability for — only these can satisfy a
   164	   * `dependsOn` (F4: a substrate or cross-rule tag never gets a status, so
   165	   * depending on one suppresses nothing, forever). */
   166	  readonly nodeTags: Set<string>;
   167	  /** Hook tags whose operand paths are syntactically clean. A path that
   168	   * failed its grammar is NOT resolved a second time — one broken
   169	   * reference must yield one problem, or a maintainer cannot tell how many
   170	   * things are actually wrong. */
   171	  readonly resolvableHooks: Set<string>;
   172	  readonly problems: string[];
   173	}
   174	
   175	/**
   176	 * A position the walk visits on only ONE channel is never evaluated on the
   177	 * other. A rule reading it there waits forever on an operand that never
   178	 * completes, and the deferred queue drops it without a word — the same
   179	 * silence, reached by a different road.
   180	 */
   181	function channelProblem(visited: readonly DeclCursor[], path: string, where: string): string | undefined {
   182	  const scoped = visited.find((cursor) => cursor.node.channel !== undefined && cursor.node.channel !== "both");
   183	  if (scoped === undefined) return undefined;
   184	  return (
   185	    `${where}: selector path ${JSON.stringify(path)} passes through ${JSON.stringify(scoped.decl)}, ` +
   186	    `which is declared channel ${JSON.stringify(scoped.node.channel)} — on the other channel that operand ` +
   187	    `is never evaluated, so the rule waits on it and is dropped in silence`
   188	  );
   189	}
   190	
   191	/** Resolve ONE selector leaf through the engine's own descent, then apply
   192	 * the gate's policy to the answer. */
   193	function leafProblems(
   194	  selector: Extract<Selector, { keysOf: string } | { valuesOf: string } | { collect: string }>,
   195	  where: string,
   196	  ctx: Context,
   197	  base: DeclCursor | undefined,
   198	): string[] {
   199	  const { relation, path } = selectorRead(selector);
   200	  const syntax = selectorPathProblems(path, where);
   201	  if (syntax.length > 0) return syntax;
   202	
   203	  const resolution = resolveSelectorPath(ctx.surface, ctx.root, base, path);
   204	  const target = resolution.visited.at(-1);
   205	  if (resolution.unreached !== undefined || target === undefined) {
   206	    return [
   207	      `${where}: selector path ${JSON.stringify(path)} addresses ` +
   208	        `${JSON.stringify(resolution.unreached ?? path)}, which the declaration does not declare`,
   209	    ];
   210	  }
   211	
   212	  const channel = channelProblem(resolution.visited, path, where);
   213	  if (channel !== undefined) return [channel];
   214	
   215	  // EVERY legal value must be readable. One that is readable for some
   216	  // inputs and silently skipped for others is what "closed" must not mean.
   217	  const needed = RELATION_READS[relation].shape;
   218	  const shapes = shapesOf(ctx.surface, target.node);
   219	  const unreadable = [...shapes].filter((shape) => shape !== needed);
   220	  if (unreadable.length === 0) return [];

    16	/**
    17	 * ADR-019 D1: THE ENGINE — one validator over declaration DATA, running
    18	 * the SAME declaration on the file-walk and direct-construction channels.
    19	 *
    20	 * The engine consumes a `SurfaceDecl`; it knows the VOCABULARY and knows
    21	 * nothing about any particular rule. A per-rule branch here (a literal
    22	 * `if (rule === X)`) is ADR-019 D9 tripwire #2 — a STOP and a scope
    23	 * review, never a work-around. Where two measured lanes disagree on a
    24	 * grain (path address, lane order, gating), the disagreement is DECLARED
    25	 * (`at:`, `laneOrder:`, `gating:`), which is the same instrument the
    26	 * audit's §5 F3 identified for path grain.
    27	 *
    28	 * Channel symmetry is structural, not argued (D1): attributes that read
    29	 * SOURCE text (`sourceForm`, the deep key-stringness scan) are declared
    30	 * once with `channel: "file"`; the engine runs them where a source exists
    31	 * and skips them where none does.
    32	 *
    33	 * This module VALIDATES and applies plain `default:` materialization. It
    34	 * does NOT derive values — derivation is the separately-named normalizer
    35	 * (D3, `normalizer.ts`).
    36	 */
    37	
    38	/** The engine's INTERNAL address for a declaration position
    39	 * (`$.steps.*.role`). It is NOT one of the declaration's three path
    40	 * languages (design review F11) — no declaration ever writes one; the walk
    41	 * builds them to key its reliability and completion bookkeeping. */
    42	type DeclPath = string;
    43	
    44	/** The channel the declaration is being run on. */
    45	export type EngineChannel =
    46	  | { readonly kind: "file"; readonly doc: Document; readonly source: string }
    47	  | { readonly kind: "direct" };
    48	
    49	export interface EngineOptions {
    50	  readonly channel: EngineChannel;
    51	  readonly catalog?: GateCatalog;
    52	}

    54	export interface EngineRun {
    55	  readonly findings: readonly ValidationFinding[];
    56	  /** The value with plain defaults materialized and file-channel maps
    57	   * realized as own-property records. Undefined when the root container
    58	   * lane failed (no operand exists). */
    59	  readonly normalized: unknown;
    60	  /** Binding path → the registration's EFFECTIVE config, collected as the
    61	   * `delegate` lanes resolve. The normalizer's `materializeEffectiveConfigs`
    62	   * hook consumes it. */
    63	  readonly effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>;
    64	  /** Every binding whose resolved registration declares
    65	   * `requiresRuntimeContext` — the operand of the R3 residual cross-rule,
    66	   * which stays hand code (audit §4). */
    67	  readonly runtimeContextBindings: readonly string[];
    68	}

    70	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    71	// Value access: ONE accessor over both channels' container forms.
    72	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    73	
    74	type AnyMap = ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> | Record<string, unknown>;
    75	
    76	function isResolvedMap(value: unknown): value is ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> {
    77	  return value instanceof Map;
    78	}
    79	
    80	/** A container map on EITHER channel: the file channel's `mapAsMap` JS Map
    81	 * or the direct channel's plain object. Arrays are never maps. */
    82	function isContainer(value: unknown): value is AnyMap {
    83	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    84	}
    85	
    86	function entriesOf(value: AnyMap): readonly (readonly [unknown, unknown])[] {
    87	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) return [...value.entries()];
    88	  return Object.keys(value).map((key) => [key, ownGet(value, key)] as const);
    89	}
    90	
    91	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    92	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    93	}
    94	
    95	/** An OWN-property lookup in a declared registry keyed by AUTHORED text.
    96	 * A step id, an event type or a scalar value may legally be `constructor`
    97	 * or `toString`, and a bracket read would return the prototype's member —
    98	 * which is not a message, and calling it as one crashes the walk. The G8
    99	 * discipline this codebase already applies to value maps, applied to the
   100	 * declaration's own maps. */
   101	function ownMessage(
   102	  registry: Readonly<Record<string, MessageTemplate>> | undefined,
   103	  key: string,
   104	): MessageTemplate | undefined {
   105	  if (registry === undefined) return undefined;
   106	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(registry, key) ? registry[key] : undefined;
   107	}
   108	
   109	function containerHas(value: AnyMap, key: string): boolean {
   110	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) return value.has(key);
   111	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, key);
   112	}
   113	
   114	function containerGet(value: AnyMap, key: string): unknown {
   115	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) return value.get(key);
   116	  return ownGet(value, key);
   117	}
   118	
   119	/** A PLAIN string-keyed map — a plain object or null-prototype object,
   120	 * never an array, a JS Map, or a class instance (`map.plain`). */
   121	function isPlainMap(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
   122	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) return false;
   123	  const proto: unknown = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
   124	  return proto === Object.prototype || proto === null;
   125	}
   126	
   127	/** Own-property WRITE: a step id or event type legally admits `__proto__`;
   128	 * a bracket assignment would set the prototype and drop the own key. */
   129	function defineOwn<T>(target: Record<string, T>, key: string, value: T): void {
   130	  Object.defineProperty(target, key, { configurable: true, enumerable: true, value, writable: true });
   131	}
   132	
   133	/** Realize a resolved value graph as own-property records where every map
   134	 * key is a string, preserving aliased identity through one memo. A map
   135	 * with any non-string key stays a JS Map (its identity class is what the
   136	 * key lanes report on). */
   137	function materialize(value: unknown, seen: WeakMap<object, unknown>): unknown {
   138	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return value;
   139	  const existing = seen.get(value);
   140	  if (existing !== undefined) return existing;
   141	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   142	    const result: unknown[] = [];
   143	    seen.set(value, result);
   144	    for (const item of value) result.push(materialize(item, seen));
   145	    return result;
   146	  }
   147	  if (isResolvedMap(value)) {
   148	    const entries = [...value.entries()];
   149	    if (entries.every(([key]) => typeof key === "string")) {
   150	      const record: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   151	      seen.set(value, record);
   152	      for (const [key, item] of entries) defineOwn(record, key as string, materialize(item, seen));
   153	      return record;
   154	    }
   155	    const map = new Map<unknown, unknown>();
   156	    seen.set(value, map);
   157	    for (const [key, item] of entries) map.set(materialize(key, seen), materialize(item, seen));
   158	    return map;
   159	  }
   160	  return value;
   161	}
   162	
   163	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   164	// THE DECLARATION RESOLVER — one model of the engine's own addressing.
   165	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166	
   167	/**
   168	 * Three review rounds in a row found the same shape of defect: the load-time
   169	 * gate kept its OWN model of which declaration positions exist and what a
   170	 * selector reaches, and that replica drifted from the walk it was modelling.
   171	 * Every fix closed one divergence and the next round found the next.
   172	 *
   173	 * So the model is here, once, and BOTH readers go through it: the runtime
   174	 * selector walk (`resolvePath`) descends with `descend`, and the gate
   175	 * resolves every declared reference with the same call. The judge and the
   176	 * executor are the same code — the drift class has nowhere left to live.
   177	 */
   178	
   179	/** One descent the walk can make. These are the only four; every address
   180	 * `evaluateNode` records is a sequence of them. */

    13	/**
    14	 * The ENGINE's own suite (ADR-019's P3 build). It tests the VOCABULARY's
    15	 * semantics over SYNTHETIC declarations — never the template
    16	 * declaration, whose correctness is measured by the 1830-case corpus the
    17	 * parity gate replayed.
    18	 *
    19	 * SENSITIVITY DISCIPLINE (process-log 2026-08-04, the non-discriminating
    20	 * fixture and the vacuous mutant): every guard below carries BOTH its
    21	 * fixture and a MUTANT declaration with that guard removed, and the
    22	 * fixture must stop producing the finding against the mutant. A fixture
    23	 * that fires either way proves nothing.
    24	 *
    25	 * Declaration-as-data makes the mutant cheap and exact: it is the same
    26	 * object with one attribute deleted, not a hand-edited copy of an
    27	 * engine. That is a property of the direction, and it is why this suite
    28	 * can afford a mutant per guard.
    29	 *
    30	 * The register is ONE level: a per-claim fixture, the name list, and a
    31	 * pinned count. No register tower (the documented anti-precedent).
    32	 */
    33	
    34	const NO_CATALOG: GateCatalog = { resolve: () => null };

    36	function surface(root: NodeDecl, extra: Partial<SurfaceDecl> = {}): SurfaceDecl {
    37	  return {
    38	    substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
    39	    root,
    40	    valueClasses: {},
    41	    crossRules: [],
    42	    normalizers: [],
    43	    ...extra,
    44	  };
    45	}
    46	
    47	function direct(
    48	  root: NodeDecl,
    49	  value: unknown,
    50	  extra?: Partial<SurfaceDecl>,
    51	  catalog: GateCatalog = NO_CATALOG,
    52	): readonly ValidationFinding[] {
    53	  return runSurface(surface(root, extra), value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog }).findings;
    54	}
    55	
    56	function fromFile(
    57	  root: NodeDecl,
    58	  yaml: string,
    59	  extra?: Partial<SurfaceDecl>,
    60	): readonly ValidationFinding[] {
    61	  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
    62	  const value: unknown = doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true });
    63	  const channel: EngineChannel = { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml };
    64	  return runSurface(surface(root, extra), value, { channel, catalog: NO_CATALOG }).findings;
    65	}

   424	export type SelectorRelation = keyof typeof RELATION_READS;
   425	
   426	/** Which relation a selector leaf names, and the path it reads. */
   427	export function selectorRead(
   428	  selector: Extract<Selector, { keysOf: string } | { valuesOf: string } | { collect: string }>,
   429	): { readonly relation: SelectorRelation; readonly path: string } {
   430	  if ("keysOf" in selector) return { relation: "keysOf", path: selector.keysOf };
   431	  if ("valuesOf" in selector) return { relation: "valuesOf", path: selector.valuesOf };
   432	  return { relation: "collect", path: selector.collect };
   433	}

   436	// Paths and message interpolation.
   437	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   438	
   439	/** `$` is the template root; `""` is a delegated config's own root. Both
   440	 * are addressed by their children's bare key (the measured path grammar). */
   441	function joinPath(parent: string, key: string): string {
   442	  return parent === "$" || parent === "" ? key : `${parent}.${key}`;
   443	}
   444	
   445	/** The list-member finding grain. Exported because the normalizer keys
   446	 * per-binding state by it and must not spell it a second way. */
   447	export function indexPath(parent: string, index: number): string {
   448	  return `${parent}[${String(index)}]`;
   449	}
   450	
   451	/** The finding-message value rendering: objects are DESCRIBED, never
   452	 * serialized (a cyclic graph reaches the walk). */
   453	function describeValue(value: unknown): string {
   454	  if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null) {
   455	    return Array.isArray(value) ? "a list" : "a map";
   456	  }
   457	  return JSON.stringify(value) ?? String(value);
   458	}
   459	
   460	/** A key rendered for a message: a string key verbatim, anything else
   461	 * through the same description ladder values use (a non-string key is a
   462	 * real possibility on the file channel). */
   463	function describeKey(key: unknown): string {
   464	  return typeof key === "string" ? key : describeValue(key);
   465	}

v3/src/definition/schema/templateSurface.ts:148:  return { findings: [], admitted: normalize(templateFormat, run.normalized, run.effectiveConfigs) };

codex
Az állapotleltár két gyanús, deklaráltan durvább riportútvonaltól független címet mutat: az open-map kulcsértékelés jelenleg a teljes map címét viszi minden kulcshoz, a lista utólagos `disjointFrom` ellenőrzése pedig minden elemhez a lista címét. Emellett az effektív konfigurációk strukturális cím helyett renderelt útvonalon vannak kulcsolva. Most mindháromhoz kapu által elfogadott, futtatható megkülönböztető próbát készítek.

+import type { GateCatalog, GateRegistration } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/ports/index.ts";
+import { closureProblems } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/defineSurface.ts";
+import { runSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts";
+import { templateFormat } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts";
+import type { NodeDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts";
+
+const rows = ["probe"] as const;
+const text = (tag: string, over: Partial<NodeDecl> = {}): NodeDecl =>
+  ({ kind: "string", tag, rows, typeMessage: "bad type", ...over }) as NodeDecl;
+const fixed = (tag: string, fields: Record<string, NodeDecl>): NodeDecl =>
+  ({ kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows, containerMessage: "bad map", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields });
+const surface = (root: NodeDecl): SurfaceDecl => ({
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [],
+});
+const run = (s: SurfaceDecl, value: unknown, catalog?: GateCatalog) =>
+  runSurface(s, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog });
+
+const catalog: GateCatalog = { resolve: (name) => name === "allowed" ? ({
+  requiresRuntimeContext: false,
+  validateAndNormalizeConfig: (raw: unknown) => ({ ok: true, effective: raw }),
+} as GateRegistration) : null };
+
+const keySurface = surface({
+  kind: "map.open", tag: "items", rows, containerMessage: "bad map", keyLaneAt: "container",
+  keyClass: text("key", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad key {valueRaw}" }, gating: true }),
+  entry: text("entry", {
+    memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { injected: "gateCatalog" }, dependsOn: ["key"], message: "bad entry {valueRaw}" },
+  }),
+});
+console.log("KEY closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(keySurface)));
+for (const value of [{ good: "ghost" }, { BAD: "ghost", good: "ghost" }, { good: "ghost", BAD: "ghost" }]) {
+  console.log("KEY", JSON.stringify(value), JSON.stringify(run(keySurface, value, catalog).findings));
+}
+
+const listSurface = surface(fixed("root", {
+  names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "names", rows, containerMessage: "bad map", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("nameValue") },
+  ids: {
+    kind: "list", tag: "ids", rows, containerMessage: "bad list", memberLaneAt: "container",
+    member: text("member", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad member {valueRaw}" }, gating: true }),
+    disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, dependsOn: ["member"], message: "collision {valueRaw}" },
+  },
+}));
+console.log("LIST closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(listSurface)));
+for (const ids of [["taken"], ["BAD", "taken"], ["taken", "BAD"]]) {
+  console.log("LIST", JSON.stringify(ids), JSON.stringify(run(listSurface, { names: { taken: "x" }, ids }).findings));
+}
+
+const delegate = (tag: string, usesTag: string): NodeDecl => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(usesTag),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}Config`, rows, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const pathSurface = surface(fixed("pathRoot", {
+  "a.b": delegate("flatBinding", "flatUses"),
+  a: fixed("nestedA", { b: delegate("nestedBinding", "nestedUses") }),
+}));
+const pathCatalog: GateCatalog = { resolve: (name) => ({
+  requiresRuntimeContext: true,
+  validateAndNormalizeConfig: () => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: name } }),
+} as GateRegistration) };
+console.log("PATH closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(pathSurface)));
+const pathRun = run(pathSurface, {
+  "a.b": { uses: "flat", config: {} },
+  a: { b: { uses: "nested", config: {} } },
+}, pathCatalog);
+console.log("PATH findings", JSON.stringify(pathRun.findings));
+console.log("PATH effective", JSON.stringify([...pathRun.effectiveConfigs.entries()]));
+console.log("PATH runtime", JSON.stringify(pathRun.runtimeContextBindings));

     1	import type { GateCatalog } from "../../ports/index.js";
     2	import type { ValidationFinding } from "../errors.js";
     3	import type { EngineChannel } from "./engine.js";
     4	import { runSurface, sourceLadderFinding } from "./engine.js";
     5	import { normalize } from "./normalizer.js";
     6	import { templateFormat } from "./templateFormat.js";
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * The template surface, composed: ONE engine run over the declaration on
    10	 * whichever channel the caller is on, plus the AUDITED RESIDUAL — the
    11	 * lanes that ADR-019 D1 leaves to prose/code — wired into the SAME finding
    12	 * pipeline so the output is one uniform stream.
    13	 *
    14	 * The residual realized here, by audit family:
    15	 *
    16	 * - **R3** the existential cross-rule over resolved registrations
    17	 *   (ch11-C19 → ch12-C5): "IF any binding resolves to a registration whose
    18	 *   `requiresRuntimeContext` is true THEN `$.runtimeContext` must be a
    19	 *   spec map" is a conditional over a DERIVED property of an injected
    20	 *   object; a `when:` general enough to express it is an expression
    21	 *   language.
    22	 * - **R7** the uses-scoped SOURCE ladder on two gate-config integers
    23	 *   (ch11-C12's source half). Its declaration exists — `[vc-authored-int]`
    24	 *   — but realizing it inside the engine requires the `delegate` hand-off
    25	 *   to carry the CHANNEL into the registration, which is a change to the
    26	 *   ratified `GateRegistration` port shape: out of ADR-019 D2's scope and
    27	 *   exactly the "while we are here" D6 forbids. Under D7's ≥2-row test the
    28	 *   alternative — a construct that scopes a source lane by a sibling's
    29	 *   value, serving this one row — is refused, and the row keeps a code
    30	 *   lane. This is R7's first live member; the family was kept open at the
    31	 *   ratification for precisely this case.
    32	 *
    33	 * Not here, and deliberately: **R5** (the store's declared-ref/filename
    34	 * check) stays the store stage's, and **R6** is library-owned wording.
    35	 */
    36	
    37	export interface SurfaceOutcome {
    38	  readonly findings: readonly ValidationFinding[];
    39	  /** The admitted VALUE, unbranded: ch11-P2a A6 keeps `admitTemplate` the
    40	   * ONLY sanctioned producer of the `AdmittedTemplate` brand, and that
    41	   * guard is lint-enforced. The engine computes the value; admission
    42	   * brands it. */
    43	  readonly admitted?: unknown;
    44	}
    45	
    46	const RUNTIME_CONTEXT_CODE = "runtime_context_required_for_process_gate";
    47	
    48	/** R7: the two uses-scoped integer fields whose SOURCE form is laddered by
    49	 * the walk. The scoping is SYNTACTIC — the authored `uses` string, no
    50	 * catalog resolution — exactly as the measured lane does it. */
    51	const SOURCE_SCOPED_CONFIG_FIELDS: Readonly<Record<string, readonly string[]>> = {
    52	  "declarative.threshold": ["value"],
    53	  "external.process": ["timeoutMs"],
    54	};
    55	
    56	function isContainer(value: unknown): value is ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> | Record<string, unknown> {
    57	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    58	}
    59	
    60	function entriesOf(
    61	  value: ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> | Record<string, unknown>,
    62	): readonly (readonly [unknown, unknown])[] {
    63	  if (value instanceof Map) return [...value.entries()];
    64	  return Object.keys(value).map((key) => [key, (value as Record<string, unknown>)[key]] as const);
    65	}
    66	
    67	function get(value: unknown, key: string): unknown {
    68	  if (!isContainer(value)) return undefined;
    69	  if (value instanceof Map) return value.get(key);
    70	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, key) ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)[key] : undefined;
    71	}
    72	
    73	/** R7's walk: `steps.<id>.gates.<event>[<i>].config.<field>` for the two
    74	 * scoped fields, on the file channel only (no source, no lane). */
    75	function sourceScopedFindings(value: unknown, channel: EngineChannel): ValidationFinding[] {
    76	  if (channel.kind !== "file") return [];
    77	  const findings: ValidationFinding[] = [];
    78	  const steps = get(value, "steps");
    79	  if (!isContainer(steps)) return findings;
    80	  for (const [stepId, step] of entriesOf(steps)) {
    81	    if (typeof stepId !== "string" || !isContainer(step)) continue;
    82	    const gates = get(step, "gates");
    83	    if (!isContainer(gates)) continue;
    84	    for (const [eventType, pipeline] of entriesOf(gates)) {
    85	      if (typeof eventType !== "string" || !Array.isArray(pipeline)) continue;
    86	      pipeline.forEach((binding: unknown, index: number) => {
    87	        if (!isContainer(binding)) return;
    88	        const uses = get(binding, "uses");
    89	        const config = get(binding, "config");
    90	        if (typeof uses !== "string" || !isContainer(config)) return;
    91	        for (const field of SOURCE_SCOPED_CONFIG_FIELDS[uses] ?? []) {
    92	          if (get(config, field) === undefined) continue;
    93	          // The AST address is built from SEGMENTS, never parsed back out
    94	          // of the rendered path: an authored step id may itself contain
    95	          // `[0]` or a dot, and re-parsing one addresses a different node
    96	          // or none at all (B1 F3 — a forbidden source form got through).
    97	          const segments = ["steps", stepId, "gates", eventType, index, "config", field];
    98	          const path = `steps.${stepId}.gates.${eventType}[${String(index)}].config.${field}`;
    99	          const node: unknown = channel.doc.getIn(segments, true);
   100	          const message = sourceLadderFinding(node, channel.source, path);
   101	          if (message !== undefined) findings.push({ path, message });
   102	        }
   103	      });
   104	    }
   105	  }
   106	  return findings;
   107	}
   108	
   109	/**
   110	 * R3: exactly ONE template-grain finding when a resolved process gate
   111	 * demands a runtime context the template does not provision. The lane is
   112	 * a DEPENDENT of the `runtimeContext` node: an illegal value fired its own
   113	 * container finding and left the normalized field absent, which suppresses
   114	 * this one (ch8-C21's container-precondition rule).
   115	 */
   116	function runtimeContextCrossRule(
   117	  normalized: unknown,
   118	  bindings: readonly string[],
   119	): ValidationFinding | undefined {
   120	  if (bindings.length === 0) return undefined;
   121	  const requirement = get(normalized, "runtimeContext");
   122	  if (requirement === undefined || isContainer(requirement)) return undefined;
   123	  return {
   124	    path: "runtimeContext",
   125	    code: RUNTIME_CONTEXT_CODE,
   126	    message:
   127	      `template declares process gate(s) requiring runtime context but does not ` +
   128	      `declare a provisionable runtimeContext spec { kind, provider } (gates: ${bindings.join(", ")})`,
   129	  };
   130	}
   131	
   132	/**
   133	 * Run the template surface on ONE channel. All-or-nothing: any finding
   134	 * means no admitted value exists (ch8-C22), and the admitted form is
   135	 * produced by the NORMALIZER, never by the validator (D3).
   136	 */
   137	export function runTemplateSurface(
   138	  value: unknown,
   139	  channel: EngineChannel,
   140	  catalog: GateCatalog,
   141	): SurfaceOutcome {
   142	  const run = runSurface(templateFormat, value, { channel, catalog });
   143	  const findings: ValidationFinding[] = [...run.findings];
   144	  findings.push(...sourceScopedFindings(value, channel));
   145	  const crossRule = runtimeContextCrossRule(run.normalized, run.runtimeContextBindings);
   146	  if (crossRule !== undefined) findings.push(crossRule);
   147	  if (findings.length > 0) return { findings };
   148	  return { findings: [], admitted: normalize(templateFormat, run.normalized, run.effectiveConfigs) };
   149	}

928:        const keyHit = findCycle(key, joinPath(path, `${segment}<key>`), ancestors);
930:        const valueHit = findCycle(child, joinPath(path, segment), ancestors);

   901	    return render(SOURCE_LADDER.resolved, { path });
   902	  }
   903	  return undefined;
   904	}
   905	
   906	
   907	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   908	// The acyclic-graph lane (the substrate's `resolve.graph`).
   909	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   910	
   911	/** DFS with a PATH-SCOPED ancestor set: shared subtrees (legal anchor
   912	 * reuse) are not cycles; only a back-edge is. */
   913	function findCycle(value: unknown, path: string, ancestors: Set<object>): string | undefined {
   914	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return undefined;
   915	  if (ancestors.has(value)) return path;
   916	  ancestors.add(value);
   917	  try {
   918	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   919	      for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i += 1) {
   920	        const hit = findCycle(value[i], joinPath(path, String(i)), ancestors);
   921	        if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
   922	      }
   923	      return undefined;
   924	    }
   925	    if (isResolvedMap(value)) {
   926	      for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   927	        const segment = typeof key === "string" ? key : "<map-key>";
   928	        const keyHit = findCycle(key, joinPath(path, `${segment}<key>`), ancestors);
   929	        if (keyHit !== undefined) return keyHit;
   930	        const valueHit = findCycle(child, joinPath(path, segment), ancestors);
   931	        if (valueHit !== undefined) return valueHit;
   932	      }
   933	    }
   934	    return undefined;
   935	  } finally {
   936	    ancestors.delete(value);
   937	  }
   938	}
   939	
   940	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   941	// Node evaluation.

   943	
   944	const DEFAULT_MISSING = 'missing required key "{key}"';
   945	
   946	/** Sibling reliability inside ONE fixed-map evaluation. `dependsOn` reads
   947	 * it before the global map so a defect in binding #1 cannot suppress a
   948	 * lane in binding #2. */
   949	function baseSlots(frame: Frame): Slots {
   950	  return { path: frame.path, value: frame.value, ownerKey: frame.ownerKey, label: frame.label };
   951	}
   952	
   953	function evaluateNode(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame): NodeResult {
   954	  const result = dispatch(run, decl, frame);
   955	  const instance = instanceKey(frame.segments);
   956	  run.completed.add(instance);
   957	  run.mark(instance, result.ok);
   958	  run.markTag(frame, decl.tag, result.ok);
   959	  return result;
   960	}
   961	
   962	function dispatch(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame): NodeResult {
   963	  switch (decl.kind) {
   964	    case "map.fixed":
   965	      return evalMapFixed(run, decl, frame);
   966	    case "map.open":
   967	      return evalMapOpen(run, decl, frame);
   968	    case "map.plain":
   969	      return evalMapPlain(run, decl, frame);
   970	    case "list":
   971	      return evalList(run, decl, frame);
   972	    case "string":
   973	      return evalString(run, decl, frame);
   974	    case "integer":
   975	      return evalInteger(run, decl, frame);
   976	    case "enum":
   977	      return evalEnum(run, decl, frame);
   978	    case "union":
   979	      return evalUnion(run, decl, frame);
   980	    case "raw":
   981	      return evalRaw(run, decl, frame);
   982	    case "valueClass":
   983	      return evalValueClassRef(run, decl, frame);
   984	    case "delegate":
   985	      return evalDelegate(run, decl, frame);
   986	  }
   987	}
   988	
   989	function evalValueClassRef(
   990	  run: Run,
   991	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "valueClass" }>,
   992	  frame: Frame,
   993	): NodeResult {
   994	  const target = run.valueClasses[decl.valueClass];
   995	  if (target === undefined) return { ok: true, value: frame.value };
   996	  const merged: Frame = { ...frame, label: decl.label ?? frame.label };
   997	  const result = dispatch(run, target, merged);
   998	  // The REFERENCING site's gating decides, not the shared class's.
   999	  if (!result.ok && decl.gating === true) run.mark(instanceKey(frame.segments), false);
  1000	  return result;
  1001	}
  1002	
  1003	function evalMapFixed(
  1004	  run: Run,
  1005	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.fixed" }>,
  1006	  frame: Frame,
  1007	): NodeResult {
  1008	  const value = frame.value;
  1009	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
  1010	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
  1011	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
  1012	    return { ok: false };
  1013	  }
  1014	  const container = value;
  1015	  // Every field position resolved ONCE, through the engine's own descent —

     1	import { indexPath, reachAll } from "./engine.js";
     2	import type { NormalizerHookDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "./vocabulary.js";
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * ADR-019 D3: THE NORMALIZER — the engine's SECOND capability, named so
     6	 * that "schema" is never read as covering it.
     7	 *
     8	 * A declaration says what is LEGAL; it does not compute a value. Admission
     9	 * both validates and PRODUCES the admitted form (the audit's residual R4:
    10	 * `advancesRound` expanded per transition, the effective gate config
    11	 * materialized). Those are TRANSFORMS, and they live here.
    12	 *
    13	 * The boundary is exact and load-bearing:
    14	 * - plain `default:` materialization IS declarable and stays SCHEMA-side
    15	 *   (the engine applies it while walking — `activation`, `runtimeContext`,
    16	 *   `output.mode`, the disposition and reason defaults);
    17	 * - DERIVATION — a value computed from other values — is the normalizer's.
    18	 *
    19	 * Each hook is a NAMED capability parameterized by declared operand paths,
    20	 * so adding one is D7 format growth (a ratified amendment), never an edit
    21	 * that slips in under "normalization".
    22	 *
    23	 * WHERE THOSE OPERAND PATHS ARE RESOLVED is not this module's business and
    24	 * no longer its code. It used to walk them with a reader of its own, which
    25	 * read `*` as a literal key and assumed a path carried exactly one of
    26	 * them; a declared path the gate had certified could therefore reach
    27	 * nothing and the hook would return having written NOTHING, without a
    28	 * word. Both hooks now walk with `reachAll` — the engine's own descent,
    29	 * the same one the gate resolves with — so a path the gate accepts is a
    30	 * path this module walks.
    31	 */
    32	
    33	/** Own-property WRITE: a step id or event type legally admits `__proto__`. */
    34	function defineOwn<T>(target: Record<string, T>, key: string, value: T): void {
    35	  Object.defineProperty(target, key, { configurable: true, enumerable: true, value, writable: true });
    36	}
    37	
    38	function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
    39	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    40	}
    41	
    42	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    43	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    44	}
    45	
    46	/**
    47	 * R4's first member (ch11-C39): expand each entry's edge map into a
    48	 * COMPLETE per-edge boolean map against the declared advancing set. An
    49	 * absent declaration yields all-false (ch11-C38's none-default); the
    50	 * PRODUCER MONOPOLY holds — the map is recomputed and overwrites whatever
    51	 * the input carried.
    52	 */
    53	function expandFlagMaps(
    54	  surface: SurfaceDecl,
    55	  root: unknown,
    56	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "expandAdvancesRound" }>,
    57	): void {
    58	  // EVERY position the operand path reaches, not the first. A wildcard
    59	  // path resolves to many, and reading only `[0]` dropped the rest with
    60	  // nothing recording it.
    61	  const advancing = new Set<string>();
    62	  for (const declared of reachAll(surface, root, hook.advanceSet)) {
    63	    if (!Array.isArray(declared.value)) continue;
    64	    for (const item of declared.value as readonly unknown[]) {
    65	      if (typeof item === "string") advancing.add(item);
    66	    }
    67	  }
    68	  for (const entry of reachAll(surface, root, hook.over, { kind: "entry" })) {
    69	    if (!isRecord(entry.value)) continue;
    70	    const edges = ownGet(entry.value, hook.edges);
    71	    const flags: Record<string, boolean> = {};
    72	    if (isRecord(edges)) {
    73	      for (const edge of Object.keys(edges)) {
    74	        defineOwn(flags, edge, advancing.has(ownGet(edges, edge) as string));
    75	      }
    76	    }
    77	    defineOwn(entry.value, hook.into, flags);
    78	  }
    79	}
    80	
    81	/**
    82	 * R4's second member (ch11-C20/C5): write each registration's EFFECTIVE
    83	 * config into the binding's single config surface. The binding is rebuilt
    84	 * from its CARRIED fields plus the effective config — the one downstream
    85	 * config form.
    86	 */
    87	function materializeConfigs(
    88	  surface: SurfaceDecl,
    89	  root: unknown,
    90	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs" }>,
    91	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
    92	): void {
    93	  for (const pipeline of reachAll(surface, root, hook.over, { kind: "entry" })) {
    94	    const owner = pipeline.owner;
    95	    const key = pipeline.key;
    96	    if (!Array.isArray(pipeline.value) || owner === undefined || key === undefined) continue;
    97	    // The per-binding key is the WALK's own finding grain, carried here
    98	    // rather than rebuilt from path text.
    99	    const rebuilt = pipeline.value.map((binding: unknown, index: number) => {
   100	      const carried: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   101	      if (isRecord(binding)) {
   102	        for (const field of hook.carry) defineOwn(carried, field, ownGet(binding, field));
   103	      }
   104	      defineOwn(carried, hook.into, effectiveConfigs.get(indexPath(pipeline.path, index)));
   105	      return carried;
   106	    });
   107	    defineOwn(owner, key, rebuilt);
   108	  }
   109	}
   110	
   111	/**
   112	 * Run every declared normalizer hook over a VALIDATED value. The caller
   113	 * guarantees the value carries no findings — a declaration that refused
   114	 * the input never reaches derivation.
   115	 */
   116	export function normalize(
   117	  surface: SurfaceDecl,
   118	  value: unknown,
   119	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
   120	): unknown {
   121	  if (!isRecord(value)) return value;
   122	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) {
   123	    if (hook.hook === "expandAdvancesRound") {
   124	      expandFlagMaps(surface, value, hook);
   125	      continue;
   126	    }
   127	    materializeConfigs(surface, value, hook, effectiveConfigs);
   128	  }
   129	  return value;
   130	}

  1442	          b: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "vb", rows: ["x"], valueClass: ok ? "idClass" : "a" },
  1443	        },
  1444	      }, { fields: { id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], valueClass: "a" } } }),

  1446	    { claim: "a grammar that is not a valid regular expression",

  1453	    it(`${guard.claim} — refused, and ONLY that`, () => {

  1456	      expect(problems[0]).toMatch(guard.problem);

  1458	    });

  1461	      expect(closureProblems(guard.make(true))).toStrictEqual([]);
  1462	      expect(() => defineSurface(guard.make(true))).not.toThrow();
  1463	    });
  1464	  }

+import type { GateCatalog, GateRegistration } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/ports/index.ts";
+import { closureProblems } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/defineSurface.ts";
+import { runSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts";
+import { templateFormat } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts";
+import type { NodeDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts";
+
+const rows = ["probe"] as const;
+const text = (tag: string, over: Partial<NodeDecl> = {}): NodeDecl =>
+  ({ kind: "string", tag, rows, typeMessage: "bad type", ...over }) as NodeDecl;
+const fixed = (tag: string, fields: Record<string, NodeDecl>): NodeDecl =>
+  ({ kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows, containerMessage: "bad map", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields });
+const surface = (root: NodeDecl): SurfaceDecl => ({
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [],
+});
+const run = (s: SurfaceDecl, value: unknown, catalog?: GateCatalog) =>
+  runSurface(s, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog });
+
+const catalog: GateCatalog = { resolve: (name) => name === "allowed" ? ({
+  requiresRuntimeContext: false,
+  validateAndNormalizeConfig: (raw: unknown) => ({ ok: true, effective: raw }),
+} as GateRegistration) : null };
+
+const keySurface = surface({
+  kind: "map.open", tag: "items", rows, containerMessage: "bad map", keyLaneAt: "container",
+  keyClass: text("key", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad key {valueRaw}" }, gating: true }),
+  entry: text("entry", {
+    memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { injected: "gateCatalog" }, dependsOn: ["key"], message: "bad entry {valueRaw}" },
+  }),
+});
+console.log("KEY closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(keySurface)));
+for (const value of [{ good: "ghost" }, { BAD: "ghost", good: "ghost" }, { good: "ghost", BAD: "ghost" }]) {
+  console.log("KEY", JSON.stringify(value), JSON.stringify(run(keySurface, value, catalog).findings));
+}
+
+const listSurface = surface(fixed("root", {
+  names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "names", rows, containerMessage: "bad map", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("nameValue") },
+  ids: {
+    kind: "list", tag: "ids", rows, containerMessage: "bad list", memberLaneAt: "container",
+    member: text("member", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad member {valueRaw}" }, gating: true }),
+    disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, dependsOn: ["member"], message: "collision {valueRaw}" },
+  },
+}));
+console.log("LIST closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(listSurface)));
+for (const ids of [["taken"], ["BAD", "taken"], ["taken", "BAD"]]) {
+  console.log("LIST", JSON.stringify(ids), JSON.stringify(run(listSurface, { names: { taken: "x" }, ids }).findings));
+}
+
+const delegate = (tag: string, usesTag: string): NodeDecl => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(usesTag),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}Config`, rows, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const pathSurface = surface(fixed("pathRoot", {
+  "a.b": delegate("flatBinding", "flatUses"),
+  a: fixed("nestedA", { b: delegate("nestedBinding", "nestedUses") }),
+}));
+const pathCatalog: GateCatalog = { resolve: (name) => ({
+  requiresRuntimeContext: true,
+  validateAndNormalizeConfig: () => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: name } }),
+} as GateRegistration) };
+console.log("PATH closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(pathSurface)));
+const pathRun = run(pathSurface, {
+  "a.b": { uses: "flat", config: {} },
+  a: { b: { uses: "nested", config: {} } },
+}, pathCatalog);
+console.log("PATH findings", JSON.stringify(pathRun.findings));
+console.log("PATH effective", JSON.stringify([...pathRun.effectiveConfigs.entries()]));
+console.log("PATH runtime", JSON.stringify(pathRun.runtimeContextBindings));

codex
Négy, kapu által elfogadott megkülönböztető eset igazolta a durvább szemcsét: open-map kulcsok közös státusza, listatagok utólagos szabályának elvesztett indexe, renderelt útvonalon ütköző effektív konfigurációk, valamint aliasolt objektum két címének közös deep-scan `seen` bejegyzése. A commit három állított javításának célzott tesztje 6/6 sikeres; most az előírt négy regressziós parancsot szigorúan sorban futtatom, a teljes tesztet önmagában.

+import type { GateCatalog, GateRegistration } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/ports/index.ts";
+import { closureProblems } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/defineSurface.ts";
+import { runSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts";
+import { templateFormat } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts";
+import type { NodeDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts";
+
+const rows = ["probe"] as const;
+const text = (tag: string, over: Partial<NodeDecl> = {}): NodeDecl =>
+  ({ kind: "string", tag, rows, typeMessage: "bad type", ...over }) as NodeDecl;
+const fixed = (tag: string, fields: Record<string, NodeDecl>): NodeDecl =>
+  ({ kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows, containerMessage: "bad map", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields });
+const surface = (root: NodeDecl): SurfaceDecl => ({
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [],
+});
+const run = (s: SurfaceDecl, value: unknown, catalog?: GateCatalog) =>
+  runSurface(s, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog });
+
+const catalog: GateCatalog = { resolve: (name) => name === "allowed" ? ({
+  requiresRuntimeContext: false,
+  validateAndNormalizeConfig: (raw: unknown) => ({ ok: true, effective: raw }),
+} as GateRegistration) : null };
+
+const keySurface = surface({
+  kind: "map.open", tag: "items", rows, containerMessage: "bad map", keyLaneAt: "container",
+  keyClass: text("key", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad key {valueRaw}" }, gating: true }),
+  entry: text("entry", {
+    memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { injected: "gateCatalog" }, dependsOn: ["key"], message: "bad entry {valueRaw}" },
+  }),
+});
+console.log("KEY closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(keySurface)));
+for (const value of [{ good: "ghost" }, { BAD: "ghost", good: "ghost" }, { good: "ghost", BAD: "ghost" }]) {
+  console.log("KEY", JSON.stringify(value), JSON.stringify(run(keySurface, value, catalog).findings));
+}
+
+const listSurface = surface(fixed("root", {
+  names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "names", rows, containerMessage: "bad map", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("nameValue") },
+  ids: {
+    kind: "list", tag: "ids", rows, containerMessage: "bad list", memberLaneAt: "container",
+    member: text("member", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad member {valueRaw}" }, gating: true }),
+    disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, dependsOn: ["member"], message: "collision {valueRaw}" },
+  },
+}));
+console.log("LIST closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(listSurface)));
+for (const ids of [["taken"], ["BAD", "taken"], ["taken", "BAD"]]) {
+  console.log("LIST", JSON.stringify(ids), JSON.stringify(run(listSurface, { names: { taken: "x" }, ids }).findings));
+}
+
+const delegate = (tag: string, usesTag: string): NodeDecl => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(usesTag),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}Config`, rows, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const pathSurface = surface(fixed("pathRoot", {
+  "a.b": delegate("flatBinding", "flatUses"),
+  a: fixed("nestedA", { b: delegate("nestedBinding", "nestedUses") }),
+}));
+const pathCatalog: GateCatalog = { resolve: (name) => ({
+  requiresRuntimeContext: true,
+  validateAndNormalizeConfig: () => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: name } }),
+} as GateRegistration) };
+console.log("PATH closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(pathSurface)));
+const pathRun = run(pathSurface, {
+  "a.b": { uses: "flat", config: {} },
+  a: { b: { uses: "nested", config: {} } },
+}, pathCatalog);
+console.log("PATH findings", JSON.stringify(pathRun.findings));
+console.log("PATH effective", JSON.stringify([...pathRun.effectiveConfigs.entries()]));
+console.log("PATH runtime", JSON.stringify(pathRun.runtimeContextBindings));

### F4 — Egy másik open-map példány completion markere eltünteti az első példány eldöntetlen szabályát

CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: A2  
WHAT: A `completed` deklarációs út szerint globális, ezért egy későbbi open-map entry jelen lévő opcionális mezője befejezettnek jelöli ugyanazt a pozíciót egy korábbi entry számára is; a korábbi deferred szabály ezután `pending` helyett `unreliable` lesz a saját hiányzó operandusán, visszatér, és az undecided-reporting queue-ba sem kerül vissza.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:570`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:665`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:747`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:1452`  
EVIDENCE:


WHY IT MATTERS: A maintainer azt látja, hogy az első entry `"ghost"` membership szabálya deklarált és nincs suppressing finding, mégis nulla findinggal admitted lesz; pusztán egy későbbi sibling entry jelenléte változtatja némává az eredményt.  
FIX SKETCH: A deferred újraértékelésben az instance-on hiányzó, egyébként megbízható operandust kezelje továbbra is `pending`/undecided állapotként, hogy a második queue-pass reportolja.

+import type { GateCatalog, GateRegistration } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/ports/index.ts";
+import { closureProblems } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/defineSurface.ts";
+import { runSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts";
+import { templateFormat } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts";
+import type { NodeDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts";
+
+const rows = ["probe"] as const;
+const text = (tag: string, over: Partial<NodeDecl> = {}): NodeDecl =>
+  ({ kind: "string", tag, rows, typeMessage: "bad type", ...over }) as NodeDecl;
+const fixed = (tag: string, fields: Record<string, NodeDecl>): NodeDecl =>
+  ({ kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows, containerMessage: "bad map", unknownMessage: "unknown", fields });
+const surface = (root: NodeDecl): SurfaceDecl => ({
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [],
+});
+const run = (s: SurfaceDecl, value: unknown, catalog?: GateCatalog) =>
+  runSurface(s, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog });
+
+const catalog: GateCatalog = { resolve: (name) => name === "allowed" ? ({
+  requiresRuntimeContext: false,
+  validateAndNormalizeConfig: (raw: unknown) => ({ ok: true, effective: raw }),
+} as GateRegistration) : null };
+
+const keySurface = surface({
+  kind: "map.open", tag: "items", rows, containerMessage: "bad map", keyLaneAt: "container",
+  keyClass: text("key", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad key {valueRaw}" }, gating: true }),
+  entry: text("entry", {
+    memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { injected: "gateCatalog" }, dependsOn: ["key"], message: "bad entry {valueRaw}" },
+  }),
+});
+console.log("KEY closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(keySurface)));
+for (const value of [{ good: "ghost" }, { BAD: "ghost", good: "ghost" }, { good: "ghost", BAD: "ghost" }]) {
+  console.log("KEY", JSON.stringify(value), JSON.stringify(run(keySurface, value, catalog).findings));
+}
+
+const listSurface = surface(fixed("root", {
+  names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "names", rows, containerMessage: "bad map", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("nameValue") },
+  ids: {
+    kind: "list", tag: "ids", rows, containerMessage: "bad list", memberLaneAt: "container",
+    member: text("member", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad member {valueRaw}" }, gating: true }),
+    disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, dependsOn: ["member"], message: "collision {valueRaw}" },
+  },
+}));
+console.log("LIST closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(listSurface)));
+for (const ids of [["taken"], ["BAD", "taken"], ["taken", "BAD"]]) {
+  console.log("LIST", JSON.stringify(ids), JSON.stringify(run(listSurface, { names: { taken: "x" }, ids }).findings));
+}
+
+const delegate = (tag: string, usesTag: string): NodeDecl => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(usesTag),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}Config`, rows, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const pathSurface = surface(fixed("pathRoot", {
+  "a.b": delegate("flatBinding", "flatUses"),
+  a: fixed("nestedA", { b: delegate("nestedBinding", "nestedUses") }),
+}));
+const pathCatalog: GateCatalog = { resolve: (name) => ({
+  requiresRuntimeContext: true,
+  validateAndNormalizeConfig: () => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: name } }),
+} as GateRegistration) };
+console.log("PATH closure", JSON.stringify(closureProblems(pathSurface)));
+const pathRun = run(pathSurface, {
+  "a.b": { uses: "flat", config: {} },
+  a: { b: { uses: "nested", config: {} } },
+}, pathCatalog);
+console.log("PATH findings", JSON.stringify(pathRun.findings));
+console.log("PATH effective", JSON.stringify([...pathRun.effectiveConfigs.entries()]));
+console.log("PATH runtime", JSON.stringify(pathRun.runtimeContextBindings));

+}));

+  console.log("LIST", JSON.stringify(ids), JSON.stringify(run(listSurface, { names: { taken: "x" }, ids }).findings));
+}

+}, pathCatalog);

+console.log("PATH runtime", JSON.stringify(pathRun.runtimeContextBindings));

codex
### F1 — Az open-map összes kulcspozíciója ugyanazt az instance-címet kapja

CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: B  
WHAT: A `keyFrame.segments` minden kulcsnál az open map címét használja, ezért egy kulcs hibás `keyClass` státusza elnyomja egy másik, jó kulcshoz tartozó entry szabályát.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:1167`  
EVIDENCE:

```text
pnpm --dir /Users/felho/dev/pairflow exec tsx /tmp/probe-grain.ts | rg '^KEY'
KEY closure []
KEY {"good":"ghost"} [{"path":"good","message":"bad entry ghost"}]
KEY {"BAD":"ghost","good":"ghost"} [{"path":"$","message":"bad key BAD"}]
KEY {"good":"ghost","BAD":"ghost"} [{"path":"good","message":"bad entry ghost"},{"path":"$","message":"bad key BAD"}]
```

WHY IT MATTERS: A maintainer tévesen azt hinné, hogy a `good` kulcs entry-jének membership lane-je a saját kulcsának státusza alapján fut; valójában az előtte bejárt `BAD` sibling dönti el, ezért még a dokumentumsorrend is megváltoztatja a választ.  
FIX SKETCH: A kulcs frame strukturális címe tartalmazza az aktuális kulcsszegmenst; a `keyLaneAt` továbbra is csak a finding renderelt útvonalát durvítsa.

### F2 — A lista utólagos `disjointFrom` ellenőrzése elveszíti a tag indexét

CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: B  
WHAT: A `clean` lista csak a renderelt pathot őrzi meg, majd minden utólagos member frame `segments` mezője a lista konténercímét kapja, ezért egy hibás tag státusza az összes tiszta tag `disjointFrom` lane-jét elnyomja.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:1270`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:1303`  
EVIDENCE:

```text
pnpm --dir /Users/felho/dev/pairflow exec tsx /tmp/probe-grain.ts | rg '^LIST'
LIST closure []
LIST ["taken"] [{"path":"ids","message":"collision taken"}]
LIST ["BAD","taken"] [{"path":"ids","message":"bad member BAD"}]
LIST ["taken","BAD"] [{"path":"ids","message":"bad member BAD"}]
```

WHY IT MATTERS: A maintainer tévesen azt hinné, hogy a tiszta `"taken"` előfordulás ütközése a saját member-státuszától függ; egy másik index hibája azonban eltünteti ezt a findingot.  
FIX SKETCH: A `clean` elem őrizze meg az eredeti strukturális `segments` címet, és az utólagos frame azt használja; az `at: "container"` csak a finding pathot szabályozza.

### F3 — Két strukturális binding-cím ugyanarra a renderelt effective-config kulcsra omlik

CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: B  
WHAT: Az `effectiveConfigs` map kulcsa `parentPath(frame.path)`, ezért a `["a.b"]` és `["a","b"]` strukturális címek ugyanazt az `"a.b"` kulcsot kapják, és a későbbi effective config felülírja a korábbit.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:611`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:1474`  
EVIDENCE:

```text
pnpm --dir /Users/felho/dev/pairflow exec tsx /tmp/probe-grain.ts | rg '^PATH'
PATH closure []
PATH findings []
PATH effective [["a.b",{"from":"nested"}]]
PATH runtime ["a.b","a.b"]
```

WHY IT MATTERS: A maintainer két sikeresen validált delegáció két effective configjára számít, de csak egy marad, így a normalizáló a másik bindinghoz rossz vagy hiányzó konfigurációt olvas.  
FIX SKETCH: Az effective config bookkeeping strukturális instance-címmel legyen kulcsolva; renderelt path csak riportoláskor készüljön.

### F4 — Az aliasolt objektum két címe közös deep-key-scan `seen` állapotot használ

CLASS: IN-SCOPE  
LENS: B  
WHAT: A `scanKeyStringness` `WeakSet<object>` kulcsa objektumidentitás, ezért ugyanaz az aliasolt map két dokumentumcímén csak az első cím non-string kulcsát jelenti.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:1158`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:1231`  
EVIDENCE:


WHY IT MATTERS: A maintainer tévesen azt hinné, hogy minden dokumentumpozíció deep key stringness lane-je lefutott; az alias második címe azonban csak az objektumazonosság miatt kimarad.  
FIX SKETCH: A deep scan ciklusvédelme az aktuális DFS-ősöket kövesse, ne a teljes bejárásban valaha látott objektumokat; ugyanazt az aliasolt objektumot másik címről újra kell vizsgálni.

- **Igen.** Négy válaszmódosító eset maradt:
  1. Az open-map key frame minden kulcshoz a map címét használja. `{BAD:"ghost", good:"ghost"}` esetén `BAD` hibája elnyomja `good` membership findingját; fordított sorrendben nem.

  3. Az `effectiveConfigs` renderelt pathon ütközik. A `["a.b"]` és `["a","b"]` binding-címekből csak az utóbbi effective config marad az `"a.b"` kulcson.
